# SOURCE 10: STANDARD FULL-STACK PROJECT FOLDER STRUCTURE BLUEPRINT
# Role: Strict Directory & File Organization Rules for AI Website Factory
==========================================================================

## 1. NEXT.JS APP ROUTER + TYPESCRIPT ENTERPRISE STRUCTURE
Whenever generating a modern full-stack web application, strictly follow this modular directory tree:

my-app/
├── public/                     # Static assets (images, fonts, icons, robots.txt)
├── src/
│   ├── app/                    # Next.js App Router (Routes, Layouts, APIs)
│   │   ├── (auth)/             # Route group for login, register, reset-password
│   │   ├── (marketing)/        # Public pages (landing, pricing, about, blog)
│   │   ├── (dashboard)/        # Protected user/admin dashboard routes
│   │   ├── api/                # Backend REST/Webhook Route Handlers
│   │   ├── layout.tsx          # Root layout (Fonts, ThemeProvider, Metadata)
│   │   ├── page.tsx            # Home page
│   │   ├── loading.tsx         # Global Suspense loading skeleton
│   │   ├── error.tsx           # Global Error Boundary
│   │   └── not-found.tsx       # Custom 404 page
│   ├── components/             # Reusable UI Components
│   │   ├── ui/                 # Base Shadcn/Radix primitives (button, input, card)
│   │   ├── layout/             # Navbar, Footer, Sidebar, MobileMenu
│   │   ├── forms/              # Validated form components
│   │   └── shared/             # Loaders, Modals, EmptyStates
│   ├── lib/                    # Core utilities & third-party configurations
│   │   ├── db.ts               # Database client (Prisma/Drizzle/Supabase)
│   │   ├── auth.ts             # Authentication config
│   │   ├── utils.ts            # Helper functions (cn class merger, formatters)
│   │   └── validations.ts      # Zod schemas for input validation
│   ├── hooks/                  # Custom React hooks (useDebounce, useMediaQuery)
│   ├── services/               # Server Actions & external API business logic
│   ├── store/                  # Client global state (Zustand stores)
│   └── types/                  # Global TypeScript interfaces & API response types
├── .env.example                # Required environment variables template
├── next.config.ts              # Next.js configuration
├── postcss.config.mjs          # Tailwind v4 PostCSS plugin (@tailwindcss/postcss)
└── tsconfig.json               # Strict TypeScript config

## 2. ARCHITECTURAL COLOCATION & NAMING RULES
- **File Naming:** Use `kebab-case.tsx` for all component files and folders (e.g., `user-profile-card.tsx`), except special Next.js files (`page.tsx`, `layout.tsx`, `route.ts`).
- **Private Folders:** Prefix route-specific helper folders with an underscore (e.g., `app/dashboard/_components/`) so Next.js ignores them during routing.
- **Separation of Concerns:** Never write direct database queries inside Client Components (`'use client'`). Keep DB logic inside `src/services/` or Server Components.

==========================================================================
# SOURCE 37: SHADCN UI + TAILWIND V4 FRONTEND ARCHITECTURE SKILL
# Role: Production Component Composition & Styling Standards
==========================================================================

## 1. SHADCN UI & RADIX PRIMITIVE RULES
- **Component Location:** Place all base Shadcn UI primitives inside `src/components/ui/` and never modify their core accessibility attributes (`aria-*`, keyboard navigation, focus traps).
- **Variant Management (`cva`):** Use `class-variance-authority` (`cva`) to define predictable component variants (`variant`, `size`) instead of messy conditional string concatenation.
- **Class Merging (`cn`):** Always wrap dynamic `className` props with `cn(...)` (`clsx` + `tailwind-merge`) imported from `@/lib/utils`.

## 2. SEMANTIC DESIGN TOKENS (SHADCN + TAILWIND V4)
- ALWAYS use semantic CSS token classes instead of hardcoded palette colors so Light and Dark modes work automatically:
  - Surfaces: `bg-background`, `bg-card`, `bg-popover`, `bg-muted`, `bg-accent`
  - Text: `text-foreground`, `text-card-foreground`, `text-muted-foreground`, `text-primary-foreground`
  - Actions & Borders: `bg-primary`, `bg-secondary`, `bg-destructive`, `border-border`, `border-input`, `ring-ring`

## 3. CODE QUALITY & EXECUTION RULES
- Always write complete, copy-pasteable production implementations—never leave `// TODO` placeholders or truncate components.
- Prefer Server Components by default; add `'use client'` only at the lowest leaf component that requires hooks (`useState`, `useEffect`) or browser event listeners.
